[Photoshoot Minigame Showcase:9] Time for some photoshoot!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:12] Hello everyone! Ace here!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:13] I wanted to show off the changes to the sprite scene interface!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:16] First up, you can already see some changes on the right of the screen.

[Sprite Interface Showcase:19] The top display has been updated to show the date and time!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:21] The clock also continues to tick as you make me do my daily activities!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:22] The current location is now shown there as well!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:25] On the bottom right, you see that the interface has changed near the dialog panel.

[Sprite Interface Showcase:26] We kept the essential buttons and added a new feature...

[Sprite Interface Showcase:28] ... my phone!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:30] There will be plenty of apps to use on it! I can't wait for you to discover them!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:33] Now to the changes on the left.

[Sprite Interface Showcase:38] This bar shows my current energy level and status.

[Sprite Interface Showcase:39] The energy level will decrease as I do my daily activities.

[Sprite Interface Showcase:43] Gosh! 

[Sprite Interface Showcase:43] I'm so tired!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:44] But if I think about sexy, naked men...

[Sprite Interface Showcase:47] Ah! That's much better!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:50] You'll also notice that the interface as a whole is smaller now, giving you more space to see the good bits.

[Sprite Interface Showcase:52] Show them fellas!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:61] Heheheeee~ I appreciate the view even more!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:70] Thanks everyone for your support, and I hope you're looking forward to the full game as much as I am!

[Sprite Interface Showcase:72] See you next time!
